## Best date plans

### 2026-10-17 — Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza + Olmsted

**Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Olmsted. It is a 6-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Grand+Army+Plaza%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=659+Vanderbilt+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/ohny-soldiers-and-sailors-memorial-arch-tours/) · [Event booking](https://ohny.org/festival) · [Restaurant](https://www.olmstednyc.com/)

#### [Pioneer Works Second Sundays](https://pioneerworks.org/programs/second-sundays-october-2026)

12:00 PM · festival · Free · Pioneer Works · Red Hook · Brooklyn · Score 75.0  
The free monthly open house spreads artist studios, workshops, exhibitions, performances, food from Dinner Belle, and bar service across Pioneer Works and its garden.
**Timing:** 12-6pm monthly open house
**Practical note:** Red Hook transit is slower than many Brooklyn venues; budget extra travel time.
**Why now:** The October Second Sundays edition falls on October 11.
**On-sale watch:** Free monthly program; registration may help if capacity is managed.

#### [Roy Lichtenstein: Like New](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/lichtenstein)

11:00 AM · museums · Free second Sunday; otherwise museum admission · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Score 69.0

**Timing:** Museum open Sunday; verify timed-entry slot when booking.
**Practical note:** Second Sunday free admission may require advance timed tickets.
**Why now:** The exhibition is newly on view during this week and Sunday is a free-admission day.
**Book soon:** Free timed-entry tickets can run out.

#### [Smorgasburg Prospect Park](https://www.smorgasburg.com/)

11:00 AM · markets · Free · Smorgasburg Prospect Park · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 65.0  
Brooklyn's open-air food market at Breeze Hill in Prospect Park, with dozens of independent vendors. Free to enter; pay per stall.
**Timing:** Sundays 11am-6pm; arrive before noon to beat the line.
**Practical note:** Entirely outdoors; check the forecast and bring water in heat.
**Why now:** Runs every Sunday of the season and pairs naturally with a Prospect Park afternoon.
[Official](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.smorgasburg.com/)

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 44.6  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

## Where to eat

Verified current NYC dining picks against captured source evidence and limited follow-up official pages, keeping useful dated offers and recent openings while removing duplicates, unsupported page-as-opening claims, and inferred dates.

Dining research: 2026-10-11T10:32:33+00:00

No candidate is kept with an unsupported Michelin-star claim. Eater heatmap inclusion is treated only as editorial selection, not an award. No Michelin Guide/star assertions were added.

### All-you-can-eat Southern-style country buffet in the East Village

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

New buffet from the teams behind Kisa and C as in Charlie serves fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rib tips, catfish, salad bar, and rotating Southern buffet dishes with unlimited return trips.

Price: Dinner $26.99 adults; $23.99 ages 65+; $13.99 kids 4-9; desserts and boozy frozen drinks separate  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5-10 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus a limited number of Resy reservations; desserts and alcoholic frozen drinks cost extra; tax, tip, and any service charge not stated.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
Published: 2026-09-11  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### One-night Chimney Rock wine dinner at The Fulton

Special menus — The Fulton

Jean-Georges seafood restaurant lists a one-night five-course prix fixe wine dinner with Chimney Rock winemaker Elizabeth Vianna, pairing dishes such as sea urchin, Dungeness crab pasta, prime New York strip, and dessert with wines.

Price: $198 five-course prix fixe  
Schedule: Wednesday, October 21, 2026 at 7:00 PM  
Conditions: Official menu says reservations; tax, tip, service charge, seating limits, cancellation policy, and whether wine pairing is included beyond the stated pairings are not stated in fetched text.  
Event date: 2026-10-21  
[Source](https://www.thefulton.nyc/menus/)

### New York African Restaurant Week three-day food and music festival

Pop-ups — New York African Restaurant Week Festival

Bounded festival offers food vendors representing Senegal, Nigeria, Ghana, Haiti, Sierra Leone, Ivory Coast, Jamaica, Trinidad, and more, plus a marketplace, live performances, and a Sunday jollof tasting contest.

Price: Event text says FREE EVENT and notes an up-to-$30 NALA food voucher while supplies last; Eventbrite structured offer data also showed a $0-$28.13 range, so retain the discrepancy rather than calling all tickets free.  
Schedule: October 9-11, 2026, 12-7 PM; broader African Restaurant Week Festival noted as October 1-17  
Conditions: Voucher requires downloading NALA app and is while supplies last; Eventbrite text says FREE EVENT, but structured offer data shows $0-$28.13 and no refunds. Active October 9-11, 2026 only. Source: https://www.eventbrite.com/e/2026-new-york-african-restaurant-week-festival-oct-9-11-tickets-1986132308199  
From: 2026-10-09  
Through: 2026-10-11  
Published: 2026-10-09  
[Source](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/2026-new-york-african-restaurant-week-festival-oct-9-11-tickets-1986132308199)

### Thomas Straker’s Soho restaurant officially opened

New openings — Straker’s

London chef Thomas Straker opened his first NYC restaurant in the former Lucky Strike space, carrying over London menu structure and dishes such as mussel flatbread with a New York ingredient focus.

Schedule: Official site lists Monday-Sunday 12-3 PM and 5-11 PM  
Conditions: Official Straker’s page confirms 59 Grand Street and Monday-Sunday hours of 12-3 PM and 5-11 PM; Eater confirms official opening Saturday, October 10, 2026. Sources: https://ny.eater.com/news/414244/strakers-thomas-straker-soho-nyc-opening and https://strakers.nyc/index.html  
Opened: 2026-10-10  
Published: 2026-10-10  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/414244/strakers-thomas-straker-soho-nyc-opening)

### Daniel Boulud returned to Lincoln Center with a large brasserie

Daniel Boulud consolidated Bar Boulud, Boulud Sud, and Épicerie Boulud energy into a two-floor French brasserie with seafood towers, French onion soup, steak frites, lobster soufflé, boudin blanc, and DB Burger.

Schedule: Eater reported opening Thursday, October 8, 2026; hours not verified

### Brazilian restaurant iara opened with Amazonian ingredients on the LES

New openings — iara

Chef Vitor Mendes’s open-kitchen Brazilian restaurant serves dishes built around cassava, tucupi, jambu, cupuaçu, picanha, moqueca, pão de queijo, and Brazilian drinks.

Schedule: Official site: Tuesday-Saturday 5-11 PM  
Conditions: Official FAQ says Resy tables are released 14 days ahead at noon for parties up to 6; walk-ins are first-come, first-served; cancellations or no-shows within 24 hours incur a $50-per-person fee; corkage is $75 per 750ml bottle. Source: https://iara.nyc/menu  
Opened: 2026-10-02  
Published: 2026-10-07  
[Source](https://iara.nyc/menu)
